# V17 paired voice comparison — part 1/3

These accepted V17 supporting registers serve the existing Humor/Voice ledger responsibilities as volume evidence appendices. Publication and whole-tranche adjudication remain separate. Original discovery, assignments and authored-time statements remain exact; current errata are explicitly external. Same-model conditional independence and receiving limits are recorded in the [V17 receiving review](../09%20Audits%20and%20Manifests/KONOSUBA_V17_RECEIVING_REVIEW.md). Exact source members are text/part0009.html (C1), part0010.html (C2), part0011.html (C3), part0012.html (F), part0013.html (Ep); excluded bodies/images/holdouts remain unopened in this continuation.

Fixed original-order records 1–81, 81 records. Order is P99 then I144, not selected by result. [Navigation/scope/exact-text catalog](KONOSUBA_V17_VOICE_COMPARISON_REGISTER.md) governs all V references. Each table value is compact JSON with one reversible HTML escape for &,<,>,|; JSON carries literal newline/backslash/quote escapes. {"@exact_text":"ID"} expands to the exact string in the named catalog. This is lossless factoring of repeated text, not a shortened rationale. Every unique initial rationale is printed in full, in its own labeled row; its value is factored out of the otherwise exact initial field bundle and restored by the inverse proof. Proof parses actual Markdown rows, reverses escapes/references, and compares every projected field to the immutable initial inputs. Private full novel bases/windows, ruby XML/pairs and long selected quotations are omitted by the declared projection; their hashes/locators and initial analytical metadata remain. No global spacing/reflow is applied to original strings. Native literal joins verify text and location, not by themselves speaker, addressee, literal-gloss accuracy or pragmatic judgment; those remain substantive receiving responsibilities.
